No. 4 ] NEW DELHI, SATURDAY, JANUARY 24 , 1976 (MAGHA 4, 1897) 
इस भाग में भिन्न पृष्ठ संख्या दी जाती है जिससे कि यह अलग संकलन के रूप में रखा जा सके

भाग III - खण्ड 4 

PART III - SECTION 4 
विधिक निकायों द्वारा जारी की गई विधिक अधिसूचनाएं जिसमें कि आदेश, विज्ञापन और सूचनाएं 

सम्मिलित हैं

Notices issued by Statutory Bodies 


- - - - -- - - - - 


भारतीय रिजर्व बैंक 

केन्द्रीय कार्यालय 

लेखा और व्यय विभाग 

बम्बई , दिनांक 24 जनवरी 1975 
जो प्रतिभतियां खो , आदि गयी है और जिनके संबंध में यह विश्वास करने के लिए प्रत्यक्षतः प्राधार है कि ये खो गयी है 
और उनके आवेदकों का दावा न्यायपूर्ण है , उनकी निम्नलिखित 30 जून , 1974 को समाप्त हुई तिमाही की सूची का विज्ञापन पब्लिक 
इंट अधिनियम , 1944 की धारा 28 के अधीन भारत सरकार द्वारा बनायी गयी और 20 अप्रैल, 1946 के भारत के राजपत्र 
में प्रकाशित ( दिनांक 29 अप्रैल , 1954 की अधिसूचना सं . एफ० ( 8 ) ( 70 ) -बी0 / 52 के अधीन संशोधित ) की गयी नियमावली 
के नियम 18 के अनुसार इसके द्वारा किया जाता है । नीचे जिन संबंधित दावेदारों के नाम दिये गये हैं उनको छोड़कर अन्य से 
सभी व्यक्तियों, जिनका इन प्रतिभूतियों पर कोई दावा हो , को चाहिए कि वे मख्य लेखाकार , भारतीय रिजर्व बैंक केन्द्रीय कार्यालय , 
लिखा और व्यय विभाग , केन्द्रीय ऋण अनुभाग , बंबई को तुरंत सूचित करें। 

यह मुची दो भागों में विभाजित की गयी है । भाग क उन प्रतिभूतियों की सूची है जिनका विजापन अभी पहली बार 
किया जा रहा है और भाग ख में उन प्रतिभनियों की सूची है जिनका पहले ही विज्ञापन किया जा चुका है । 

- सूचो क

संख्या रूपय ग्राम

1976 की प्रस्तावित साधारण सभा में बैंक के संचालक मंडल 
में संचालक निर्वाचित हुए मान लिये जायेंगे । अतः सह सभा उक्त 
नियमन के नियम 33 ( 1 ) के अंतर्गत अब निरस्त हो गयी है ।

की अधिसूचना दी जाती है : - -

दिनांक 27 जन , 1975 से केन्द्रीय कार्यालय के अन्तर्गत अंतर्राष्ट्रीय 
विभाग में क्रमशः महाप्रबन्धक तथा उप महाप्रबन्धक का पद 
भार ग्रहण कर लिया है ।

एक्काऊन्टन्ट रैगर ले शन्स , 1958 में निम्न संशोधन किये है जो 
उपरोक्त धारा की उपधारा ( 3 ) के प्र. नुसार पहले ही प्रकाशित 
हुमा औरजिसका केन्द्रीय सरकार ने अनुमोदन किया है । 

काहे हुये रेग्युलेशन्स में -- 

I. वर्तमान रेग्युलेन्शन 25 के लिये निम्नलिखित 
नया रेग्युलेशन्स स्था नापन्न किया जायेगा । 

" 25. परीक्षाओं और रजिस्ट्रेशन रद्द करने के लिये 
समय- सोमा --- 
( 1 ) एक रजिस्टर्ड छन को रजिस्ट्रेशन के दिन से सात 
वर्ष की अवधि के अन्दर इस संस्था के इन्टरमीडिएट और फ इनल 
परीक्ष ओं को साधारणतः पूरा करना होगा इसमें असफल होने 
पर उपका रजिस्ट्रेशन रद्द होने की संभ बना रहेगी । 

( 2 ) एक छात्र यदि वह अपने रजिस्ट्रेशन के दिन से तीन 
वर्ष की अवधि के भीतर इन्टरमीडिएट परीक्षामें बैठने में असफल 
होता है तो उसका रजिस्ट्रेशन रद्द होने की भी संभावना 
रहेगी । 

उपधारा ( 2 ) उन लोगों के लिये प्रयोज्य नहीं होगा 
जो अप्रल , 1976 के पहले रजिस्टर्ड छ. स हो जायेगा । 
. II: वर्तमान रेग्युलेशन 146 के लिये निम्नलिखित 
स्थानापन्न किया जायेगा : - - 

" 146 : कास्ट एक्काऊन्टेन्ट्स के अध्याय : 

परिषद् इस संस्था के जर्नल में अधिसूचना देकर कास्ट 
एक्काऊन्टैन्ट्स के अध्यायों को बना सकता है और इस उद्देश्य से 
इस सम्बन्ध के उपनियमों को बना सकता है । 

पूर्ववर्ती अधिकारों के सर्वसाधारण नियम के बिना विपरीत 
प्रभाव के ऐसे उपनियम अध्यायों को बनाने की परिस्थिति उत्पन्न 
कर सकते हैं । 
. इस प्रकार बनाया गया अध्याय सदा क्षेत्रीय परिषद , जो 
उस क्षेत्र में कार्य करता है इसके नियंत्रण,निरीक्षण और निदेशन 
के अनुसार कार्य करेगा और क्षेत्रीय परिषद् के द्वारा समय 
समय पर प्रकाशित वैसे निदशों को पूरा करेगा । 
. परिषद् किसी समय यदि ऐसा करना आवश्यक विचार करेगा 
तो किसी अध्याय को प्रकरण को कहने की सुविधा देकर हटा 
सकता है । 

. . . दिनांक 15 दिसम्बर 1975 

सं० सी० डबल्यू ०आर० ( 3 )/ 75 -- दी कॉस्ट एण्ड वसं 
एक्काऊन्टैन्ट्स रंग्युलेशन , 1959 में दी कॉस्ट एण्ड वस 
एक्काऊन्टेन्ट्स अधिनियम , 1959 की धारा 39 की उप-धारा 


( 1 ) और ( 3 ) 1959 के अधिनियम संख्या 23 के द्वारा दिये 
गये अधिकारों का प्रयोग करते हुये कछ संशोधनों का निम्न प्रारूप 
सभी लोगों की सूचना के लिये प्रकाशित किया जाता है । इससे 
प्रभावित सभी लोगों को सूचना दी जात है कि प्रारूप पर 30 
जनवरी , या उर के बाद विचार किया जायेगा । . 

दी इन्ट . ट अॉफ प्रॉस्ट एप्ड वसं एक्याऊन्टैन्ट्स 
अॉफ इण्डिया : परिषद् के द्वारा उपरत प्रारूप के रग्बन्ध 
में किसी शक्ति रस प्रकार की पत्ति या राय लिपित

following list (for the quarter cnded 30th June, 1974 ) is hereby advertised of securities lost etc . in respect of which prima lacic grounds 
exist for believing that the securities have been lost and that the claim of applicants is just. All persons other than the respective claimants 
named below who have any claim upon these securities should communicato immediately with the Chief Accountant, Reserve Bank of 
India , Central Office , Department of Accounts and Expenditurc , Central Debt Section , Bombay. 

The list has been divided into two parts — Part A being securities now advertised for the first time and Part B the list of securities 
previously advertised .

Explanatory note on the above amendments : 

The following is the Explanatory Note on the amendments 
proposed in Notification No. CWR ( 3 ) /75 dated 15th Decem 
ber 1975 . This note is intended only lo clarify the intention 
of the Council underlying these amendments and should not 
be construed as limiting or amplifying the scope of these 
amendments in any manner whatsoever . 

Irem I. In the Cost and Works Accountants Act and in the 
Cost and Works Accountants Regulations the term " Constitu 
encies " has been used . For sake of uniformity thc word 
" Regions is being changed to " Constituencies" , in Regula 
tion 54 . 

Items II & III . Once the election programme is decided 
upon by the Council, tbc publication of Voters List and of the 
Notice calling for nominations of candidates for election is 
the responsibility of the Secretary . 

The present requirement of the Regulations is that notice 
calling for nominations etc , is to be published in the Journal of 
the Institute . Since the date of issuing notice and the date of 
publication of the Journal may not coincide , it has been 
decided to publish the notice in the Journal as routine but not 
as statutory obligation . The list of Voters and a copy of the 
clection notice shall be sent to each member of the Institute 
as a statutory obligation . 

Item V . According to existing Regulation 60 ( 1 ) an appcal 
against the Secretary s refusal or rejection or acceptanco of 
a nomination may be preferred to the Council . Since the 
Council cannot mcet within the short time available under 
the Election Regulations to refer such appeal to the Appeal 
Committee , it has been decided that the Secretary should have 
the authority to refer the appeal to the Appeal Committee 
without waiting for the Council to takc action . Hence the 
guggested amendment. 

1401 VI. According to existing Regulation 63 E a candidate 
is entitled to appoint any number of persons as his authorised 
representatives to be present on his behalf at the polling 
booths. The proposed amendment secks to restrict the 
appointment of not more than two persons for cach polling 
booth and such persons shall be members of the Institute . 

Item VII. The printed Voters Lists are so narrowly spaced 
that they cannot be conveniently used for obtaining signatures 
of the voters. It is proposed that separate forms will be used 
for taking the signatures of the voters . Also , verification with 
the specimen signature is being dispensed with us many voters 
do not send their specimen signaturcs . 

Item VIII . Since Section 10 ( 2 ) of the Act providçs for 
dealing with clcction disputes , the proviso to Regulation 69A 
is redundant and hence is being deleted. 

S. N . GHOSE , Secy . 


UNIT TRUST OF INDIA 

New Delhi, the 31st December 1975 
No. UT. 7437 /NP48- 75 / 76 .- The Unit Scheme, 1976 
made by the Board of Trustees of thc Upit Trust of India 
under Section 21 of the Unit Trust of India Act, 1963 , at its 
meeting held on 2nd December 1975 is published herewith for 
general information : 

UNIT SCHEME, 1976 
In exercise of the powers conferred by Sectiva 21 of the 
Unit Trust of India Act, 1963 (52 of 1963) , the Board of 
the Unit Trust of India hereby makes the following Unit 
Scheme : 

1. Short title and commencement. - - ( 1 ) This Scheme shall 
be called the Unit Scheme, 1976 . ( 2 ) It shall come into force 
on January 1, 1976 . 

2 . Objective . — This Scheme primarily aims at securing for 
the unit holders capital appreciation on their investment in 
Units by investing the funds of the scheme in equity shares and 
convertiblc bonds / debentures of companies with good growth 
prospects. 

3 . Definitions. In this Scheme, unless the context other 
wisc rcqpuires 

( a ) the " Act" means the Unit Trist of India Act, 1963; 
( b ) " acceptance dale " with reference to an application 

made by an applicant to the Trust or its agent duly 
authorised in this bebalf for sale or repurchase of 
units by the Trust means the day on which the Trust 
or its authorised agent, as the case may be, after 
being satisfied that such application is in order, 

acccpts the same; 
( c ) " body corporate " includes a society registered under 

the Societies Registration Act, 1860 , such society 

being hereinafter referred to as " a socicty " ; 
( d ) " eligible trust" shall have the mcaning assigned to 

it under clausc (aan ) of Regulation 2 of the Regu 

lations . 
( e ) “ Regulations" means Unit Trust of India General 

Regulations, 1964 made under Section 43 ( 1 ) of the

Provided that during a period of one month from 
the date on which the sale of units under this scheme 
commences , the Trust may sell units at the face value 

of Rs. 100 / - per unit. 
( 2 ) The price at which a Unit will be sold by the Trust 

(hereinafter referred to as " the sale prico " ) , and the 
price at which a unit will be repurchased by the 
Trust (hereinafter referrred to as " the repurchase 
price " ) shall be determined by the Trust on every 
Tuesday and Friday ( or, the next working day , il 
any of these days happens to be a holiday ) , and 
shall aply to sales and repurchases between the open 
ing of business on the next day and the close of 
business of the succeeding Friday or Tuesday , as the 
case inay be , ( or , the next working day if that Friday 

or Tuesday is a holiday ) . 
( 3 ) Thc sale price shall be arrived at by dividing the 

value ( determined as hereinafter indicated ) as at 
the close of business on the working day immediate 
ly preceding the day on which the sale price is deter 
mined , of the assets pertaining to this scheme, reduced 
by liabilities pertaining to this scheine, not being 
contingent liabilities or liabilties in respect of the 
unit capital including reserves , if any, as at the 
close of business on the said working day , by the 
number of units in issue as at the close of business 
on the said day, adding thereto such sum as in the 
opinion of the Trust is adequate to cover brokernge , 
commission , taxes, if any, stamp duties , other charges 
in relation to the acquisition of investments by the 
Trust and management expenses and adjusting lip 
wards the resulting price by not more than 10 paisc 

per unit. 
( 4 ) The purchase price shall be arrived it by dividing 

the value ( determined us hereinafter indicated ) as a 
the close of business on the working day inmediate 
ly preceding the day on which the repurchase price 
is determined , of the assets pertaining to this scheme, 
reduced by liabilities pertaining to this schene, not 
being contingent liabilities or labilities in respect of 
the unit capital including reserves , if any, as at the 
close of business on the said working day by the 
tiumber of units in issue as at the close of business 
on the said day , declucting therefrom such sum as in 
the opinion of the Trust is adequate 10 cover broke 
age, commission . taxes, if any stamp dut es and 
other charges in relation to the realisation of invest 
ments by the Trust and adiusting downwards the 

resulting price by not more than 10 paise per unit. 
( 5 ) The sale price or the repurchase price of a unit shall 

be arrived at on the basis of the material available 
with the Trust on the day on which the sale price , or 

the repurchase price , as the case may be, is arrived at. 
(6 ) Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in sub 

clauses ( 2 ) , ( 3 ) , ( 4 ) and ( 5 ) , when thc Trust is 
satisfied jhat it is in the interest of ihc Trust and the 
un t holders to vary the sale or repurchase price or 
both , 15 arrived at under sub - clause ( 3 ) or, as the case 
may be, sub - clause ( 4 ) , in the event of a complete 
breakdown or dislocation of business in the financial 
market due to war, insurrection , civil commotion or 
any other serious or sustained political and industrial 
disturbance or if the whole present basis of stock 
cxchange prices should undergo a substantial change 
through the occurrence of some catastrophe or 
similar event, it may vary the sale or repurchase 

price or both to such an extent as it may deem fit .

Scheinc may be suspended or closed by the Trust at any time 
after giving notice of seven days in important daily news 
papers of its intention to do so . 

32. Cony of scheme to be made available . — A copy of this 
scheme incosnorating all amendments thereto shall be made 
available for inspection at the oſlices of the Trust at all times 
during its business hours . 


25. Nomination by Unit Holders and 1 genis. — Unit holders 
and agents may exercise the right to make or cancel a nomi 
nation to the extent provided in the Regulations. 
26 . Payments to unitholders : 
( 1 ) The income to be distributed to the unit holders 

shull be paid , as soon as may be , after the closing of 
the annual accounts, as on the 30th Junc of eachi 

year. 
( 2 ) It shall be lawful for a unit holder to receive and 

retain any income declared by the Trust in respect of 
units of which he is such holder , notwithstanding that 
the units have already been transferred by him for 
consideration unless the transfcrec who claims the 
income from the transferor has within fifteen days 
of the date on which the income becamc duc , lodged 
the certificate and all other documents rclatitig to the 
transfer which may , under the provisions of Clause 
23 or otherwise , be required by the Trust, for being 
registered in his name.
